### [94] [Reconfigurable Current Distributions in Percolating Au Films and CrO2 Powder Compacts - Beyond Geometrical Connectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20285)
*E. Yu. Beliayev,I. G. Mirzoiev,V. A. Horielyi*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该文比较半连续Au薄膜与压片CrO₂粉末两种体系，提出"电学相关网络"是物理接触网络中依赖条件的子集，并归纳出"链路启用"与"链路重加权"两种可互转的输运重构模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Au薄膜与CrO₂压片在形貌、维度与微观输运机制上差异极大，但都可能因几何连通性不足以解释强非均匀导体的输运行为。作者试图寻找跨体系的网络层级共性原理。

Method: 对比分析两类体系的电输运响应：Au薄膜在几何逾渗阈值附近改变偏压（低温响应由激活型最近邻跳跃伴随负磁阻，转向类弱局域化伴随正磁阻）；CrO₂压片通过磁场改变既有晶间结的自旋相关电阻，并由温度与测量电流扩展或耗尽活跃网络。同时引入Cohn定理作为灵敏度判据。

Result: Au薄膜中偏压可逆地改变参与导电的网络子集（岛网络更广泛的电子参与）；CrO₂压片中磁场主要重排竞争电流路径，温度与电流可调节活跃网络范围。Cohn定理表明局部电阻变化对宏观响应的贡献正比于该链路所载电流。

Conclusion: 提出两种极限但可相互转换的重构模式——链路启用（link enabling）与链路重加权（link reweighting），并指出仅凭几何连通性无法解释强非均匀导体的输运，需考虑条件依赖的电学活跃网络。

Abstract: Despite fundamental differences in morphology, dimensionality, and microscopic transport, semicontinuous Au films and compacted CrO2 powders converge on a common network-level principle: the electrically relevant network is a condition-dependent subset of the physical contact network. In Au films near the geometrical percolation threshold, increasing bias reversibly shifts the low-temperature response from activated nearest-neighbour hopping with negative magnetoresistance to a weak-localization-like regime with positive magnetoresistance, consistent with broader electronic participation of the fixed island network. In CrO2 powder compacts, magnetic field mainly changes the spin-dependent resistances of existing intergranular junctions and thereby reorders competing current paths; temperature and measuring current can additionally expand or deplete the active network. Cohn's theorem provides a compact sensitivity principle for both cases: local resistance changes influence the measured response in proportion to the current carried by the affected links. The comparison identifies two limiting but interconvertible modes of reconfiguration - link enabling and link reweighting - and shows why geometrical connectivity alone is insufficient for interpreting transport in strongly inhomogeneous conductors.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.str-el'></div>

# cond-mat.str-el [[Back]](#toc)

### [95] [Bad metallicity in the semi-quantum regime of the Hubbard model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19264)
*Evyatar Tulipman,Vadim Oganesyan,Thomas P. Devereaux,Steven A. Kivelson*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在无限U哈伯德模型低空穴密度下，用精确对角化发现一个类似液氦"半量子区"的中间温区：压缩率近似T无关，而电阻率呈T线性并超过Ioffe-Regel极限，其微观机制是掺杂空穴周围形成准局域铁磁"自旋笼"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 坏金属普遍表现出超越Ioffe-Regel极限的近似T线性直流电阻率，且该行为出现在基态迥异的多种体系中，这暗示它是强局域关联的普适表现。作者希望检验这一假说，并寻找其微观机制。

Method: 采用无限U哈伯德模型、小空穴掺杂密度，对有限尺寸团簇进行精确对角化，计算热力学与输运性质随温度的变化。

Result: 降温过程中出现一个中间温区，量子效应使压缩率近似与T无关，但电阻率呈T线性且超过Ioffe-Regel极限。该温区伴随掺杂空穴周围形成准局域铁磁"自旋笼"，使局域量子运动嵌入涨落自旋背景中。

Conclusion: 该中间温区是液氦"半量子区"的电子类比，其中瞬态"晶笼"控制非相干输运。尽管模型极简，所得到的坏金属行为与多种材料平台中的观测相似，支持坏金属是强局域关联普适表现的假说。

Abstract: Bad metals exhibit approximately $T$-linear dc resistivity beyond the Ioffe--Regel limit. That this behavior occurs in systems with radically different ground states suggests it is a generic manifestation of strong local correlations. We test this hypothesis in the infinite-$U$ Hubbard model with small hole densities using exact diagonalization to compute thermodynamic and transport properties of finite clusters. Upon cooling, we find an intermediate temperature range, an electronic analogue of the ``semi-quantum regime'' of liquid helium, in which quantum effects produce a roughly $T$-independent compressibility, yet the resistivity is $T$-linear and exceeds the Ioffe--Regel limit. Entry into this regime is accompanied by the formation of quasi-local ferromagnetic ``spin cages'' around doped holes, regions that facilitate local quantum motion embedded in a fluctuating spin background, analogous to the transient crystalline cages thought to control incoherent transport in semi-quantum liquid helium. Remarkably, despite the simplicity of the model, the bad metal behavior found here resembles that seen in various material platforms.

</details>


### [96] [Non-Abelian Anyon Condensation: a Path-Integral Monte Carlo Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19282)
*Rafael Flores-Calderón,Frank Pollmann,Michael Knap*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作为Kitaev量子双模型建立无符号问题的路径积分框架，通过非可逆1-形式对称性统一描述非阿贝尔任意子凝聚、禁闭与广义对称破缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非阿贝尔拓扑序之间的相变在微观量子模型中难以用可扩展的数值方法描述，缺乏可控的大尺度计算框架。

Method: 将单键微扰按非可逆电/磁1-形式对称性组织，使不同任意子物种增殖；对任意有限群G构造精确的"matterization"等距映射，引入顶点自由度并将纯键模型映射为G规范-希格斯理论；利用不动点的1-形式对称性定义广义Fredenhagen-Marcu序参量，并开展量子蒙特卡洛模拟。

Result: 对G=S_3，非阿贝尔电任意子增殖驱动一级相变，所有非平凡电任意子同时凝聚；在纯磁极限下模型退化为(2+1)维纯G规范理论，对S_3展现一级禁闭相变，可由Wilson环面积律的出现及涌现磁1-形式对称性的恢复来诊断。

Conclusion: 该框架为非阿贝尔任意子凝聚、禁闭和广义对称破缺提供了统一的数值研究途径。

Abstract: Transitions out of non-Abelian topological order are difficult to describe in microscopic quantum models with numerical methods that remain tractable at large scales. We develop a sign-free path-integral framework for Kitaev quantum doubles $\mathcal D(G)$ by organizing single-link perturbations in terms of non-invertible electric and magnetic 1-form symmetries that proliferate distinct anyon species. For any finite group $G$, an exact $\textit{matterization}$ isometry introduces vertex degrees of freedom and maps the link-only model onto a $G$ gauge--Higgs theory. Furthermore, the 1-form symmetries of the fixed point allow us to define generalized Fredenhagen--Marcu order parameters that become finite when the corresponding anyons condense. For $G = S_3$, quantum Monte Carlo simulations show that proliferating a non-Abelian electric anyon drives a first-order transition in which all nontrivial electric anyons condense. In the purely magnetic limit, the model reduces to a $(2+1)$D pure $G$ gauge theory; for $G=S_3$, it exhibits a first-order confinement transition, diagnosed by the onset of a Wilson-loop area law and the restoration of an emergent magnetic 1-form symmetry. These results provide a unified numerical framework for non-Abelian anyon condensation, confinement, and generalized symmetry breaking.

</details>


### [97] [Valley-Helical Superconductivity Driven by Repulsion and Quantum Geometry: Applications to Time-Reversal Symmetric Rhombohedral Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19283)
*Julian May-Mann,Trithep Devakul,Gal Shavit*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在无谷极化、无时间反演破缺的体系中，过屏蔽（Kohn-Luttinger）机制可在两个谷中诱导手性相反的配对，形成"谷螺旋"超导态，并在中等耦合下战胜区间谷配对态；在时间反演对称的菱面体石墨烯模型中，该态在4≤N≤8的各层数下均占据主导填充与位移场区间，并表现出拓扑投影边缘模、反常磁响应及交织的Kekulé型键序等特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受石墨烯与过渡金属硫族化合物体系中手性超导实验迹象的启发，作者追问：手性配对是否必须在谷极化或时间反演对称破缺的前提下才能出现，即能否在保持时间反演对称、无谷极化的条件下实现手性配对。

Method: 针对两谷具有相反Berry曲率的体系，采用Kohn-Luttinger过屏蔽排斥机制分析配对不稳定性；对比谷螺旋态与常规区间谷配对态在弱耦合与中等耦合下的竞争；并使用时间反演对称的菱面体石墨烯实际模型，计算不同层数（4≤N≤8）下各填充与位移场条件下的主导超导态。

Result: 过屏蔽排斥可在两谷中诱导手性相反的配对，形成谷螺旋态；该态在中等耦合下能量上优于更常规的区间谷配对态，尽管后者才具有弱耦合不稳定性。在菱面体石墨烯模型中，谷螺旋超导在4≤N≤8所有层数下均存在可观的填充与位移场主导区间。该态还表现出拓扑投影边缘模、非常规磁响应以及交织的Kekulé型键序。

Conclusion: 时间反演对称且无谷极化的体系中同样可以存在手性配对，谷螺旋超导是一种可在多层菱面体石墨烯中广泛实现的候选态，其边缘模、磁响应与键序特征可作为区别于常规超导体的实验判据。

Abstract: Motivated by the recent experimental signatures of chiral superconductivity in valley-polarized graphene- and transition-metal-dichalcogenide-based systems, we investigate the possibility of chiral pairing in the absence of valley polarization or time-reversal symmetry breaking. For systems with opposite Berry curvature in the two valleys, we find that overscreened repulsion (i.e., the Kohn-Luttinger mechanism) can indeed induce pairing of opposite chiralities in each valley, producing a valley-helical state. Moreover, the valley-helical state can outcompete more conventional intervalley paired states at intermediate coupling, despite only the latter having a weak-coupling instability. Using a realistic model of time-reversal-symmetric rhombohedral graphene, we find extended ranges of fillings and displacement fields with dominant valley-helical superconductivity. This occurs for all layer numbers considered here, $4\leq N\leq 8$. We show that the valley-helical states can display topologically projected edge modes, unusual magnetic responses, and intertwined Kekulé-like bond-order; features that can be used to distinguish them from more conventional superconductors.

</details>


### [98] [Energy partitioning of wave packets in one-dimensional systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19450)
*Flavia B. Ramos,Imke Schneider,Sebastian Eggert,Rodrigo G. Pereira*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文研究一维临界费米子系统中的波包非平衡动力学及能量在一维激发模式间的分配，发现相互作用导致初态激发分数化为反向传播的左右移动模式，且能量分配非普适，依赖于注入波包的细节（如宽度）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨非平衡态下波包激发的动力学行为及其能量在涌现激发模式间的分配规律，并与电荷分数化进行对比，以揭示能量分配是否具有普适性。

Method: 在少体基态上注入高斯波包，利用时间依赖的密度矩阵重整化群（TD-DMRG）追踪其实时演化；并采用Luttinger液体理论推导涌现模式所携带能量的解析预测。

Result: 相互作用使初始激发分数化为反向传播的左右移动模式，其能量可在实空间分辨。低能区域场论预测与数值模拟吻合良好。与完全由Luttinger液体参数决定的电荷分数化不同，能量分配是非普适的，取决于注入波包宽度等细节。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了能量分配在实空间中的表征，并建立了非平衡数值模拟与有效场论描述之间的定量比较。

Abstract: We investigate the non-equilibrium dynamics of wave packets in a one-dimensional critical fermionic system and analyze the resulting partitioning of energy between emergent excitations. Starting from a Gaussian wave packet injected on top of the many-body ground state, we follow its real-time evolution using the time-dependent density-matrix renormalization group. We observe that interactions lead to fractionalization of the initial excitation into counter-propagating left- and right-moving modes, whose energies can be resolved in real space. To interpret these results, we employ Luttinger liquid theory, which allows us to derive analytical predictions for the energy carried by the emergent modes. We find good agreement between field-theoretical predictions and numerical simulations in the low-energy regime. In contrast to charge fractionalization, which is completely determined by the Luttinger liquid parameter, we show that energy partitioning is non-universal and depends on details of the injected wave packet, such as its width. Our results provide a real-space characterization of energy partitioning in one-dimensional systems and establish a quantitative comparison between non-equilibrium numerical simulations and the effective field-theory description.

</details>


### [99] [Universality of the $1/9$ Magnetization Plateau and Quantum-Disordered States in the Kagome Family $\mathrm{Cs_8AB_3Ti_{12}F_{48}}$ ($A=\mathrm{Rb},\mathrm{Li}$; $B=\mathrm{K},\mathrm{Na}$)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19572)
*Prena Chaudhary,Asiri Thennakoon,Tommy Park,Hanru Wang,Leshan Zhao,Laurel Winter,Neil Herrison,Christina Hoffmann,Junghong H. He,Harald O. Jeschke,Hiroyuki Nojiri,Akira Matsuo,Koichi Kindo,Miwako Takahashi,Yukio Noda,Taku J. Sato,Shiyan Li,Hiroaki Ueda,Gia-Wei Chern,Seung-Hun Lee*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过化学压力调控钛基kagome体系Cs8AB3Ti12F48，发现1/9磁化平台在晶格膨胀时稳健存在而1/3平台缺失；压缩则破坏该平台与量子无序基态。结果表明1/9与1/3平台微观起源不同，1/9平台可能是阻挫spin-1/2 kagome磁性的更普遍特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: spin-1/2 kagome反铁磁体中低场1/9磁化平台的微观起源仍未解决，需要新的调控手段与实验证据来厘清其与常规1/3平台的关系。

Method: 对钛基kagome家族Cs8AB3Ti12F48（A=Rb,Li；B=K,Na）施加化学压力（改变A、B离子实现晶格膨胀或压缩），开展最高60 T的高场磁化测量，并结合比热测量与第一性原理计算。

Result: 晶格膨胀的Cs8RbK3Ti12F48和Cs8LiK3Ti12F48中稳健出现1/9平台样相，却缺失通常更稳健的1/3平台；压缩的Cs8LiNa3Ti12F48既无1/9平台样相也无量子无序基态。比热与第一性原理计算表明，晶格膨胀保持阻挫、全连接的kagome交换网络及无能隙量子无序基态，而压缩将交换网络重组为弱耦合的准一维子系统并诱发连续磁相变。

Conclusion: 1/9与1/3平台不必共享同一微观起源，1/9平台可能是阻挫spin-1/2 kagome磁性更普遍的特征。

Abstract: The microscopic origin of the low-field $1/9$ magnetization plateau in spin-$1/2$ kagome antiferromagnets remains unresolved. Here, we show that chemical pressure reshapes the hierarchy of fractional magnetization plateaus in the titanium-based kagome family $\mathrm{Cs_8AB_3Ti_{12}F_{48}}$ ($A=\mathrm{Rb},\mathrm{Li}$; $B=\mathrm{K},\mathrm{Na}$). High-field magnetization measurements up to 60 T reveal a robust $1/9$ plateau-like phase in the expanded $\mathrm{Cs_8RbK_3Ti_{12}F_{48}}$ and $\mathrm{Cs_8LiK_3Ti_{12}F_{48}}$ compounds, despite the absence of the conventionally more robust $1/3$ plateau. In contrast, compressed $\mathrm{Cs_8LiNa_3Ti_{12}F_{48}}$ exhibits neither the $1/9$ plateau-like phase nor a quantum-disordered ground state. Specific-heat measurements and first-principles calculations show that lattice expansion preserves a frustrated, fully connected kagome exchange network and gapless quantum-disordered ground states, whereas compression reorganizes the exchange network into weakly coupled quasi-one-dimensional subsystems and induces successive magnetic transitions. These results demonstrate that the $1/9$ and $1/3$ plateaus need not share a common microscopic origin and suggest that the $1/9$ plateau may represent a more universal feature of frustrated spin-$1/2$ kagome magnetism.

</details>


### [100] [Vestigial chirality from fluctuating loop currents on the kagome lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19618)
*Yin Shi,Xuande Bu,Sheng Meng*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了kagome格子上公度三Q环流序的分阶段熔化行为，通过蒙特卡洛模拟揭示直接熔化与两阶段熔化两种机制，并建立定量畴壁判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多分量序可以分阶段熔化，在主要成分变为短程后仍保留复合序。作者希望探究这种可能性是否适用于kagome格子上公度三Q环流序，并为近期kagome金属中在常规电荷密度波临界温度之上观测到的时间反演奇响应提供可能的热力学框架。

Method: 在有效固定振幅符号模型上开展大规模团簇蒙特卡洛与并行回火模拟；进行有限尺寸标度分析；并构建稳定的振幅分辨Ginzburg-Landau理论以检验振幅弛豫后相的稳定性。

Result: 模拟揭示了直接熔化与两阶段熔化两种机制。在两阶段机制中，平移扇区畴壁先于手性改变畴壁增殖，从而恢复晶格平移对称性，同时保留长程时间反演奇序；该中间相中主要M点关联为短程。双阶段机制在最低能量手性改变畴壁仅比同手性平移畴壁贵约12%–13%时启动。有限尺寸标度表明该相在振幅弛豫下依然存在。

Conclusion: 研究确立了残余环流序的定量畴壁判据，以及一条无需长程环流布拉格序即可破坏时间反演对称性的涨落路径。这种分离为kagome金属中近期报道的时间反演奇响应提供了可能的热力学框架。

Abstract: Multicomponent order can melt in stages, leaving a composite order after its primary constituents become short ranged. We study this possibility for commensurate three-$Q$ loop-current order on the kagome lattice. Large-scale cluster and parallel-tempering Monte Carlo simulations reveal direct and two-stage melting regimes in an effective fixed-amplitude sign model. In the latter regime, translation-sector domain walls proliferate before chirality-changing walls, restoring lattice translational symmetry while preserving long-range time-reversal-odd order. The primary $M$-point correlations are short ranged in this intermediate phase. The two-stage regime begins when the lowest-energy chirality-changing wall is only about $12$--$13\%$ more costly than a same-chirality translation wall. Finite-size scaling of a stable amplitude-resolved Ginzburg--Landau theory shows that the phase survives amplitude relaxation. Our results establish a quantitative domain-wall criterion for vestigial loop-current order and a fluctuation route to time-reversal symmetry breaking without long-range loop-current Bragg order. This separation provides a possible thermodynamic framework for time-reversal-odd responses recently reported above the critical temperature for conventional charge-density-wave ordering in kagome metals.

</details>


### [101] [Fractional quantum spin Hall crystals from hidden density-wave order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19638)
*Matthew Shammami,Sudip Chakravarty*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出对称性破缺可以生成拓扑态：混合单-三重态d密度波作为粒子-空穴凝聚体，其隐藏键序破缺平移对称性并产生互为时间反演的Chern带对，在奇数分母分数填充下经排斥相互作用可转变为分数量子自旋霍尔液体，并呈现电荷-自旋交叉响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对称性破缺与拓扑通常被视为描述物质自组织方式的两种独立框架。作者希望探究二者之间是否存在生成关系，即一种机制能否催生另一种，从而统一理解强关联体系中对称破缺序与拓扑序的关联。

Method: 提出混合单-三重态d密度波（一种粒子-空穴凝聚体）模型，其隐藏键序破缺平移对称性但不产生常规电荷或自旋密度序，并生成一对时间反演的Chern带。采用有限尺寸精确对角化方法，在奇数分母分数填充下研究排斥相互作用对该对称破缺拓扑态的影响。

Result: 排斥相互作用可将对称破缺拓扑态转变为分数量子自旋霍尔液体，其相反手性产生交叉响应：电荷流输运自旋、自旋流输运电荷。同时揭示了该相的边界：强自旋间耦合与更稀填充倾向电荷序并使残余带色散起决定作用；半填充下自旋退耦极限给出均匀关联液体，其信号与复合费米液体物理一致。

Conclusion: 对称性破缺与拓扑并非互斥描述，隐藏键序可生成拓扑带结构，并进一步在相互作用下演化为分数量子自旋霍尔液体，展现电荷-自旋交叉输运；同时该框架划分了电荷序、复合费米液体等相邻相的边界。

Abstract: Broken symmetry and topology are distinct descriptions for how matter organizes itself. Here, we propose one gives rise to the other. A mixed singlet-triplet $d$-density wave, which is a particle-hole condensate, has a hidden bond order that breaks translation symmetry and generates a time-reversed pair of Chern bands, without producing conventional charge or spin density order. At fractional fillings with odd denominators, finite size exact diagonalization studies show how repulsive interactions can transform this symmetry-broken topological state into a fractional quantum spin Hall liquid. Its opposite chiralities produce a crossed response in which charge flux transports spin, and spin flux transports charge. The same setting also reveals what borders such a phase. Strong interspin coupling and increasingly dilute fillings favor charge order and make residual band dispersion decisive, while at half filling, the spin-decoupled limit gives a homogeneous correlated liquid with signatures consistent with the physics of a composite Fermi liquid.

</details>


### [102] [Pressure induced structural phase transition and magneto-elastic coupling in Eu doped LaCrO$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19795)
*Asish Kumar Mishra,Mrinmay Sahu,Bhagyashri Giri,Bidisha Mukherjee,Suvashree Mukherjee,Harekrishna Bhunia,Tamalkanti Mukherjee,Sujoy Ghosh,Partha Mitra,Peter Liermann,Goutam Dev Mukherjee*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本研究对5% Eu掺杂LaCrO₃(ELCO)开展高压同步辐射XRD、微区拉曼光谱与低温磁化测量，揭示~10.6 GPa处正交→菱方结构相变，~4.5 GPa处拉曼异常，并预测压力诱导磁基态转变为反铁磁有序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究压力下ELCO的结构与磁性关联，并与未掺杂LaCrO₃(LCO)的类似结果对比，理解Eu掺杂及压力对磁基态的影响。

Method: 高压同步辐射X射线衍射(XRD)、微区拉曼光谱、低温磁化测量三者结合，分析结构、声子与磁性随压力的演化。

Result: XRD显示约10.6 GPa发生正交→菱方结构相变，拉曼数据佐证该结果并表明低频软模引起的晶格不稳定性驱动相变；~4.5 GPa处多个拉曼模的位移与积分强度出现显著异常。

Conclusion: 结合低温磁化与XRD、拉曼异常，并类比LCO，预测压力诱导磁基态转变为反铁磁有序。

Abstract: In this study, we have carried out a detailed high pressure investigation on 5$\%$ Eu doped LaCrO$_3$ (ELCO) using synchrotron X-ray diffraction (XRD), micro Raman spectroscopy, and low-temperature magnetization measurements to correlate the structural and magnetic properties under pressure. The high pressure XRD reveals the orthorhombic to rhombohedral structural phase transition around 10.6 GPa. The high pressure Raman data corroborate this result and indicate that the lattice instabilities associated with the low-frequency soft modes play a crucial role in driving this transition. In addition, a pronounced anomaly in the Raman shift and integrated intensity of several Raman modes is observed around 4.5 GPa. By combining the low-temperature magnetization measurements with the anomalies observed in the XRD and Raman data and by comparing with the similar results in LaCrO$_3$ (LCO), a pressure-induced change in the magnetic ground state to antiferromagnetic ordering is predicted.

</details>


### [103] [Tunable flat band on the surface of a rhombohedral kagome ferromagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19857)
*Xinglu Que,Qingyu He,Lihui Zhou,Lilian Prodan,Vladimir Tsurkan,István Kézsmárki,Hidenori Takagi,Dennis Huang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在层状kagome铁磁体Fe3Sn2中，STM观测到近费米能级的平带；第一性原理计算表明该平带源于菱方堆垛kagome层间的层间跳跃而非层内跳跃的相消干涉，且局限于表面层，位于布里渊区角点K̄/K̄'附近，并表现出由谷对称性破缺和轨道磁矩导致的磁场响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索kagome基材料的核心目标之一是实现带宽约为meV量级、且靠近费米能级的平带。此前的主流假设认为平带起源于kagome晶格上层内跳跃的相消干涉（destructive hopping）。作者希望验证这一假设，并厘清Fe3Sn2中平带的真实起源与形成机制。

Method: 对层状kagome铁磁体Fe3Sn2进行扫描隧道显微术（STM）测量，观测其微分隧道电导谱；结合第一性原理slab（薄板）计算，分析能带扁平化的来源（层内vs层间跳跃）、空间分布（体态vs表面态）及轨道特征；进一步在磁场下测量并计算能带的磁响应，包括谷对称性破缺引起的精细结构与轨道磁矩导致的抗磁位移。

Result: STM证实Fe3Sn2在费米能级附近存在平带，表现为微分隧道电导中的尖锐峰。计算揭示该平带并非由层内跳跃的相消干涉导致，而是由Fe3Sn2中菱方堆垛kagome层之间的层间跳跃所致，且定域于表面层。该表面带形成于布里渊区角点K̄和K̄'附近，表现出丰富的磁场依赖行为：包括旋转Fe磁矩破坏谷对称性产生的精细结构，以及伴随轨道磁矩的持续抗磁位移，计算结果均予以重现。

Conclusion: 研究强调了层堆垛方式对kagome磁体能带结构的关键作用，并在实验上展示了在拓扑磁体原子级薄层体积中产生可磁调控平带的替代机制。

Abstract: A central goal in the exploration of kagome-based materials is the realization of a flat band that has meV bandwidth and lies close to the Fermi energy. The prevailing assumption is that band flattening originates from destructive hopping processes on the kagome lattice. We perform scanning tunneling microscopy (STM) on the layered kagome ferromagnet Fe$_3$Sn$_2$ and show that it indeed hosts a flat band near the Fermi energy, which is manifested as a sharp peak in the differential tunneling conductance. First-principles slab calculations reveal, however, that this band is flattened not by the destructive interference of intralayer hopping, but by interlayer hopping between rhombohedral-stacked kagome planes in Fe$_3$Sn$_2$, and is confined to the surface layer. This surface band, forming in the vicinity of the Brillouin zone corners $\bar{K}$ and $\bar{K'}$, exhibits rich magnetic-field dependence, including fine structure due to valley-symmetry breaking by rotated Fe moments, as well as a persistent diamagnetic shift associated with orbital magnetic moments, all reproduced by our calculations. Our results, highlighting the crucial role of layer stacking on the band structure of kagome magnets, demonstrate experimentally an alternative mechanism of generating magnetically tunable flat bands in atomically thin volumes of topological magnets.

</details>


### [104] [Corner entanglement scaling with projected entangled pair states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20020)
*Chloé Van Bastelaere,Rui-Zhen Huang,Laurens Vanderstraeten*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在热力学极限下从投影纠缠对态(P EPS)直接提取角纠缠熵的方法，发现角贡献随有效关联长度标度，符合有限纠缠标度假设，角系数与其他方法一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 角状二分下的纠缠贡献在二维量子临界点具有由共形场论决定的普适标度行为，可作为探测普适性质的强有力探针，但如何在热力学极限下高效提取该角纠缠熵仍具挑战。

Method: 开发了一种在热力学极限下直接从投影纠缠对态(P EPS)中提取角纠缠熵的方法。

Result: 在量子临界点模型上应用时，角贡献随有效关联长度呈现标度行为，与有限纠缠标度假设一致；所得角系数与其他方法相符。

Conclusion: 该方法可高效诊断二维强关联量子临界点。

Abstract: Entanglement scaling provides a powerful probe of universal properties at quantum critical points. In two dimensions, contributions originating from a corner-shaped bipartition exhibit a universal scaling, which is determined by the underlying conformal field theory. We develop a method to extract this corner entanglement entropy from projected entangled pair states directly in the thermodynamic limit. When applied to models at a quantum critical point, we show that the corner contribution exhibits scaling with the effective correlation length, in agreement with the hypothesis of finite-entanglement scaling. Our results for the corner coefficients are consistent with other methods, demonstrating the efficiency of our method for diagnosing strongly-correlated quantum critical points in two dimensions.

</details>


### [105] [When Is Kramers-Wannier Duality Invertible?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20090)
*Akash Sinha,Pramod Padmanabhan,Vladimir Korepin*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文刻画了有限希尔伯特空间上 Kramers-Wannier 对偶何时能实现为可逆幺正变换，并给出了基于 Ising 键代数两个中心元的充要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子 Ising 链的 Kramers-Wannier 对偶在有限周期链上自然表现为非可逆变换，只有在引入对称性扭曲扇区后才能提升为可逆幺正变换。此前缺乏一个统一判据来判断对偶何时具有可逆实现，本文旨在填补这一空白并统一已有构造。

Method: 将问题归结为 Ising 键代数的表示问题，利用该代数的两个中心元给出可逆实现的表示论判据，该判据同时涵盖常规非可逆实现与含对称性扭曲扇区的可逆构造。

Result: 证明了对偶是否可逆实现由 Ising 键代数的表示决定，并建立了关于其两个中心元的充要条件；该判据对具有相同底层键代数的其他模型同样适用。作为一个显式例子，在一些 Ising 键代数的非平凡实现中发现了允许可逆 Kramers-Wannier 对偶的序-无序对偶。

Conclusion: Kramers-Wannier 对偶的可逆实现由一个统一的表示论判据支配，该判据推广并统一了非可逆与可逆（扭曲扇区）两类实现，并可应用于更广泛的同键代数模型。

Abstract: {\it Kramers-Wannier} duality of the quantum Ising chain is naturally realized as a noninvertible transformation on a finite periodic chain. Upon incorporating appropriate symmetry-twisted sectors, however, the duality can be promoted to an invertible unitary transformation. In this Letter, we classify when the Kramers-Wannier duality admits an invertible realization on a finite Hilbert space. We show that this is determined by the representation of the Ising bond algebra and establish a necessary and sufficient condition in terms of its two central elements. This representation-theoretic criterion unifies the conventional noninvertible realization with invertible constructions involving symmetry-twisted sectors and applies equally to other models with the same underlying bond algebra. As an explicit example, we find an order-disorder duality in a non-trivial realization of the Ising bond algebra that admits an invertible Kramers-Wannier duality.

</details>


### [106] [Pressure-induced electronic and structural evolution of EuIrGe3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20376)
*N. S. Dhami,V. Baledent,I. Batistic,C. M. N. Kumar,D. Kaczorowski,L. Nataf,J. M. Ablett,J. -P. Rueff,J. P. Itie,P. Fertey,S. R. Shieh,P. Popcevic,Y. Utsumi Boucher*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 高压下EuIrGe3呈现连续Eu价态升高与38 GPa以上结构相变，反铁磁基态在18 GPa内保持稳健，其压力响应区别于EuCoGe3、EuRhGe3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究非中心对称BaNiSn3型反铁磁体EuIrGe3在压力下的电子与晶体结构演化，并厘清过渡金属d电子在EuTGe3家族压力响应中的作用，解释其与EuCoGe3、EuRhGe3的差异。

Method: X射线吸收谱（Eu L3边）、同步辐射X射线衍射结合密度泛函理论计算，以及电阻率测量。

Result: Eu L3边谱显示平均Eu价态随压缩连续升高，并伴随Ge、Ir电子态改变；高压XRD显示四方I4mm相各向异性压缩，并在38 GPa以上出现结构相变；实验晶格与状态方程参数与DFT吻合；电阻率显示反铁磁有序温度在18 GPa内单调上升。

Conclusion: EuIrGe3的压力响应不同于EuCoGe3和EuRhGe3，凸显过渡金属d电子态在EuTGe3家族压力诱导电子与结构演化中的关键作用。

Abstract: We investigated the pressure-induced evolution of the electronic and crystal structure of the noncentrosymmetric BaNiSn3-type antiferromagnet EuIrGe3 using x-ray absorption spectroscopy, synchrotron x-ray diffraction complemented by density functional theory calculations, and electrical resistivity measurements. The Eu L3-edge spectra reveal a continuous increase in the mean Eu valence under compression, accompanied by modifications of the Ge and Ir electronic states. High- pressure x-ray diffraction shows anisotropic lattice compression in the tetragonal (I4mm) phase and provides evidence for a structural phase transition above 38 GPa. The experimentally deter- mined lattice and equation-of-state parameters are in good agreement with the DFT calculations. Electrical resistivity measurements reveal a monotonic increase in the antiferromagnetic ordering temperatures up to 18 GPa, indicating that the antiferromagnetic ground state remains robust despite the increasing contribution of the nonmagnetic Eu3+ configuration to the intermediate va- lence state. These results demonstrate that EuIrGe3 exhibits a pressure response distinct from EuCoGe3 and EuRhGe3, highlighting the important role of the transition metal d-electron states in the pressure-induced electronic and structural evolution of the EuT Ge3 family

</details>


### [107] [Emergent Surface Kondo Flat Band Driven by Competing Interactions in a Topological Ferromagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20432)
*Nazar Zaremba,Susmita Changdar,Haojie Guo,Iñigo Robredo,Daniel Lozano-Gomez,Andreas Leithe-Jasper,Rui Lou,Yurii Prots,Mitja Krnel,Markus König,Konstantin Semeniuk,Berit H. Goodge,Yuri Grin,Andrii Kuibarov,Oleksandr Suvorov,Alexander Fedorov,Adam Pikul,Ivan Soldatov,Rudolf Schäfer,Bernd Büchner,Oksana Kvitnitskaya,Yurii Naidyuk,Louis Lamm,Meike Pfeiffer,Elena Hassinger,Matthias Vojta,Sergey Borisenko,Miguel M. Ugeda,Jeroen van den Brink,Eteri Svanidze,Maia G. Vergniory*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作发现了一种新现象：磁性可驱动同一材料中不同区域共存不同的强关联电子态，即"关联量子相的自发空间分离"，以UAsS单晶为平台建立了组织量子物质的新机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代凝聚态物理的核心目标之一是揭示由强电子关联、磁性与能带拓扑相互交织所产生的新奇量子态。重费米子相源于Kondo相互作用，是电子关联最显著的体现之一，拓扑重费米子态已在若干非磁性材料中被确认；然而它们与磁序竞争所产生的后果在很大程度上仍未得到探索。

Method: 结合体敏感探测手段（输运等）表征UAsS晶体的体态性质；通过第一性原理计算预测其拓扑电子结构；利用角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）验证体能带中的节点线与Weyl点；并采用表面敏感的ARPES以及扫描隧道显微镜/谱（STM/STS）探测表面电子态。

Result: 体敏感测量表明UAsS晶体在体内是金属性铁磁体，仅具有中等的关联驱动能带重整化。第一性原理计算揭示其拓扑电子结构同时包含节点线和Weyl点，指向丰富的拓扑性质，ARPES测量与之相符，分辨出节点线和Weyl交叉点。与此形成鲜明对比，表面敏感的ARPES和STM/STS测量揭示出一个钉扎在费米能级处的显著平带，并伴随一个尖锐共振——这是第一性原理计算未能捕捉到的、涌现出的强关联Kondo态的标志。

Conclusion: 磁性可驱动不同的强关联电子态在同一材料的不同区域共存，从而确立了"关联量子相自发空间分离"这一此前未知的组织量子物质机制，为关联拓扑体系的研究开辟了新方向。

Abstract: A central goal of modern condensed matter physics is to uncover new quantum states of matter arising from the intertwined effects of strong electron correlations, magnetism, and band topology. Heavy-fermion phases, generated by Kondo interactions, represent one of the most remarkable manifestations of electronic correlations, and topological heavy-fermion states have been identified in several non-magnetic materials. Yet, the consequences of their competition with magnetic order have remained largely unexplored. Here, we reveal a new phenomenon: the spontaneous spatial separation of correlated quantum phases. By showing that magnetism can drive distinct strongly correlated electronic states to coexist in different regions of a single material, our work establishes a previously unknown mechanism for organizing quantum matter and opens a new direction in the study of correlated topological systems. Using \emph{bulk-sensitive} probes, we show that UAsS crystals are, in the bulk, metallic ferromagnets with only moderate correlation-driven band renormalizations. First-principles calculations reveal a topological electronic structure hosting both nodal lines and Weyl points, pointing to a rich underlying topology. Angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES) measurements are consistent with these predictions, resolving the nodal lines and Weyl crossings. In striking contrast, \emph{surface-sensitive} ARPES and scanning tunneling microscopy/spectroscopy (STM/STS) measurements reveal a pronounced flat band pinned at the Fermi level, accompanied by a sharp resonance -- hallmarks of an emergent, strongly correlated Kondo state not captured by first-principles calculations.

</details>


### [108] [Non-Thermal Effects in Fermionic Atoms Coupled to Open Cavities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20434)
*Jules Sueiro,Marco Schiro*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 研究费米-哈伯德模型与耗散腔模耦合，用Keldysh图解推导并求解量子动理学方程，发现无相互作用时费米子达到由非热分布函数刻画的非平衡稳态，并使费米子与腔模间热流为零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索开放腔模作为非平衡噪声与耗散源时，费米子系统的稳态性质、有效温度描述及超辐射相变的平均场相图如何受涨落影响。

Method: 将玻色腔模视为非平衡噪声和耗散的来源，采用Keldysh图解（非平衡格林函数）推导并求解费米子的量子动理学方程；先处理无哈伯德相互作用情形，再在弱耦合区加入哈伯德相互作用。

Result: 无相互作用时费米子普遍达到非平衡稳态，具有非热分布函数，且该分布恰好使费米子与腔模间热流为零；给出低频有效温度描述出现的参数区域，说明涨落对超辐射相变平均场相图的影响；弱耦合哈伯德相互作用导致向完全平衡分布的渡越（crossover）。

Conclusion: 开放腔模驱动的费米子系统可呈现非热但零热流的稳态，并可在特定参数区用有效温度刻画；相互作用驱动系统向平衡分布渡越，表明耗散腔模与相互作用共同决定稳态与相变行为。

Abstract: We study a Fermi-Hubbard model coupled to a open dissipative single cavity mode. Using Keldysh diagrammatics we derive and solve the quantum kinetic equations for the fermions, taking the bosonic cavity mode as a source of non-equilibrium noise and dissipation. In absence of Hubbard interactions we show that the fermions reach generically a non-equilibrium steady-state, characterized by a non-thermal distribution function. Quite interestingly we demonstrate that the latter exactly nullifies the heat-current between fermions and cavity mode. We discuss the regimes of parameters where a low-frequency effective temperature description emerges and how the fluctuations affect the mean-field phase diagram for the superradiance phase transition. Finally we include Hubbard interaction in the weak-coupling regime and show that it leads to a crossover towards a full equilibrium distribution.

</details>


### [109] [Collective Charge-\(2e\) Bosonic Excitations in Charge-Ordered Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20472)
*Ping Tang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文在有效电荷-赝自旋模型中构建电荷序的集体激发，通过Holstein-Primakoff变换得到两支带相反量子化电荷±2e的玻色型准粒子"双荷子"(bichargons)，并揭示其热扩散产生电荷Seebeck响应、在交流电场下可参数化激发并支持直流漂移电流等行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电荷序通常仅以电子密度的静态调制来描述，其集体激发从准粒子角度的发展远不如自旋序系统（如反铁磁体中自旋波/磁振子）成熟。作者希望像磁振子范式那样，为电荷序建立一套集体玻色型载流子的准粒子图像。

Method: 构建有效电荷-赝自旋模型，用交错赝自旋序表示电荷序基态；对有序态周围的涨落进行Holstein-Primakoff变换以量子化，得到玻色型集体激发谱；再考察温度梯度、化学势调节、静态电场与交流电场下的输运响应。

Result: 发现两支简并、有能隙的玻色型准粒子分支，携带相反量子化电荷±2e，命名为"bichargons"，与二分反铁磁体两支携带相反自旋角动量的磁振子分支相平行（也可由Jin et al., PRL 2015的电荷-赝自旋模型理解）。温度梯度驱动热激发双荷子扩散，当通过化学势调节偏离电荷中性点、破坏两支简并时产生电荷Seebeck响应。虽然双荷子携带有限电荷，但因其准粒子数不守恒，在静态电场下热双荷子气体仍呈绝缘性。交流电场则可参数化地产生成对带相反电荷的双荷子，在偏置电场下支持有限的直流漂移电流，类似半导体中光激发电子-空穴对介导的光电导。

Conclusion: 双荷子被确立为电荷序系统中一类新的集体玻色型电荷载流子，将自旋序系统中磁振子的准粒子范式推广到电荷部门，其中被输运的量由自旋角动量替换为电荷。这为电荷序集体激发的准粒子描述提供了新框架。

Abstract: Charge order is conventionally characterized by a static modulation of the electronic density, while its collective excitations remain less developed from a quasiparticle perspective than those of spin-ordered systems. Here, we formulate the collective excititations of charge order within an effective charge-pseudospin model, in which the charge-ordered ground state is represented by staggered pseudospin order. Quantizing fluctuations around this ordered state via a Holstein--Primakoff transformation, we reveal two branches of bosonic quasiparticles with degenerate, gapped dispersions that carry opposite quantized electric charges $\pm 2e$. We therefore term these charge-$2e$ bosons ``\textit{bichargons},'' closely paralleling the two magnon branches of a bipartite antiferromagnet that carry opposite spin angular momenta. We show that a temperature gradient drives the diffusion of thermally excited bichargons, generating a charge Seebeck response when the degeneracy between the oppositely charged branches is lifted by tuning the chemical potential away from the charge-neutrality point. In contrast, despite carrying finite electric charges, a thermal bichargon gas remains electrically insulating under a static electric field because bichargon quasiparticle number is not conserved. An ac electric field, however, can parametrically generate coherent pairs of oppositely charged bichargons that support a finite dc drift current in the presence of a bias electric field, offering a bosonic analogue of photoconductivity mediated by photoexcited electron--hole pairs in semiconductors. Our results establish bichargons as a new class of collective bosonic charge carriers in charge-ordered systems and extend the quasiparticle paradigm of magnons in spin-ordered systems to the charge sector, with electric charge replacing spin angular momentum as the transported quantity.

</details>


### [110] [Unconstrained compact lattice QED$_{2+1}$ coupled to phonons: Gauss sectors, orthogonal semimetal, and deconfined criticality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20502)
*João C. Inácio,Fakher F. Assaad*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过无约束紧致格点QED₂₊₁耦合键声子的模型，研究高斯定律动力学生成以及退禁闭、对称破缺与单极子竞争之间的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规范理论描述二维量子磁体时需在高斯定律约束的物理希尔伯特空间上定义，但高斯算符本征值如何动力学地被选定、这一过程与禁闭及对称破缺的关系尚不清楚。

Method: 采用N_f=2的精确辅助场量子蒙特卡洛模拟，研究无约束紧致格点QED₂₊₁耦合键声子系统，分析物理高斯扇区的能量选择、显式施加高斯定律后的单极子激发及相图。

Result: 识别出正交半金属（OSM）相，其中费米子剥离规范荷形成狄拉克液体，混合不同高斯扇区并在显式施加高斯定律时失稳；施加高斯定律后紧致性允许携带AFM和VBS量子数的单极子激发，规范涨落与自旋子-声子耦合调控单极子竞争，给出OSM、AFM、VBS相图。

Conclusion: OSM相变伴随高斯定律的动力学生成，AFM与VBS带电单极子竞争产生与退禁闭量子临界一致的转变，表明投影到物理希尔伯特空间后，紧致格点QED₂₊₁中的退禁闭总是不稳定地趋向AFM或VBS序。

Abstract: Gauge theoretic descriptions of 2D quantum magnets are defined on a restricted physical Hilbert space by imposing Gauss's law. In this work, we study an unconstrained compact lattice QED$_{2+1}$ coupled to bond phonons, in which local Gauss operators are conserved, but their eigenvalues are not a priori fixed. Using exact auxiliary-field quantum Monte Carlo simulations at $N_f = 2$, we investigate how the physical Gauss sector becomes energetically favourable, and how this process is connected to confinement and symmetry breaking. We identify an orthogonal semimetallic (OSM) phase, in which fermions striped off their gauge charge and form a Dirac liquid. This phase mixes various Gauss sectors, and becomes unstable when Gauss's law is explicitly enforced. Upon the imposition of Gauss's law, compactness allows for monopole excitations carrying antiferromagnetic (AFM) and valence-bond-solid (VBS) quantum numbers. Gauge field fluctuations and spinon-phonon coupling tune between the competition of such monopoles and generate a phase diagram with OSM, AFM and VBS orders. The transitions out of the OSM phase coincide with the dynamical generation of Gauss's law, while the competition between AFM and VBS charged monopoles produces a transition consistent with deconfined quantum criticality. Thus our results establish that upon the projection to the physical Hilbert space, deconfinement in compact lattice QED$_{2+1}$ is always unstable towards either AFM or VBS order.

</details>


### [111] [Multiconfigurational Analysis of Local Electronic Structure of $\mathrm{RuO_2}$ Using Relativistic Embedded Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20526)
*Zhosan I. A.,Lomachuk Yu. V.,Maltsev D. A.,Moiseev I. A.,Andreev O. Yu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文用多组态相对论嵌入簇方法研究RuO₂局域电子结构，发现其4d轨道能谱与劈裂更接近四方D₄h对称性而非形式上的正交D₂h，轨道准简并抑制了xy四极序，从而解释了块体RuO₂中未观测到交变磁性、而应变薄膜中却出现该现象的原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RuO₂是交变磁性候选材料，但块体实验未观测到交变磁性的自旋劈裂信号，而应变薄膜中却报告了稳健信号，这一矛盾需要从局域电子结构层面给出解释。独立粒子模型认为交变磁性依赖正交对称性导致的xy四极序，因此需检验真实晶体环境中Ru位点的轨道能谱与对称性行为。

Method: 采用多组态、相对论嵌入簇方法，从自由Ru离子出发，逐步构建Ru+Q₆静电模型、裸[RuO₆]⁸⁻配体模型，以及高精度RuO₆@CTEP嵌入簇以重现晶体环境；所有体系均在SA-CASSCF和NEVPT2+SOC水平处理，以同等精度描述强电子关联与自旋轨道耦合。

Result: 尽管RuO₂中Ru位点的形式局域对称性为正交D₂h，计算得到的4d轨道能谱及其劈裂模式却更接近更高的四方D₄h对称性，相关4d轨道间保持强准简并。

Conclusion: 独立粒子模型中交变磁性自旋劈裂所依赖的xy四极序需要对称性降低，而轨道准简并会抑制该对称性降低，这自然解释了块体RuO₂实验中未观测到交变磁性，而与应变薄膜中稳健交变磁性信号形成对比。

Abstract: We present a multiconfigurational, relativistic embedded-cluster study of the local electronic structure of ruthenium dioxide ($\mathrm{RuO_2}$), a candidate altermagnetic material. Starting from free $\mathrm{Ru}$ ions, we progressively build up the local environment through a $\mathrm{Ru+Q_6}$ electrostatic model, a bare $\mathrm{[RuO_6]^{8-}}$ ligand model, and finally a high-accuracy $\mathrm{RuO_6}$@CTEP embedded cluster that reproduces the crystalline surroundings. All systems are treated at the SA-CASSCF and NEVPT2+SOC levels of theory to capture strong electron correlation and spin-orbit coupling on an equal footing. While the formal local site symmetry of the $\mathrm{Ru}$ sites in $\mathrm{RuO_2}$ is orthorhombic ($D_{2h}$), we find that the calculated $4d$-orbital energy spectrum and its splitting pattern behave much closer to the higher tetragonal ($D_{4h}$) symmetry, preserving a strong quasi-degeneracy among the relevant $4d$ orbitals. Since the local $xy$ quadrupolar order responsible for altermagnetic spin splitting in independent-particle models relies on this symmetry reduction, its suppression by orbital quasi-degeneracy offers a natural explanation for why altermagnetism is not observed in bulk $\mathrm{RuO_2}$ experiments, in contrast to the robust altermagnetic signatures reported in strained thin films.

</details>


### [112] [Lyapunov-controlled thermalization: an exact real-time example](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20597)
*Jonas Loy,Jan C. Louw*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文证明KMS关系成立不足以判定平衡，通过大N大q SYK双淬火协议揭示隐藏的初始态记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验"淬火后满足KMS关系即证明系统达到平衡"这一常见判据是否充分。

Method: 在大N、大q极限下构造SYK模型的淬火协议（淬火后回到初始哈密顿量的二次淬火），精确求解完整的非平衡实时演化。

Result: 淬火后所有费米子关联函数严格为热化形式，但二次淬火后出现隐藏的初始态记忆，编码在与第一次淬火前时间相连的关联函数中；该记忆是广延的非平衡见证量，其衰减率即为Lyapunov指数λ_L。

Conclusion: KMS关系满足不构成平衡的充分证据；λ_L决定了态在何种速率下变得与Gibbs态有效不可区分。该可精确求解的幺正相互作用多体系统为解析研究热化提供了理想平台。

Abstract: A verified Kubo-Martin-Schwinger (KMS) relation, after a drive has ceased, is not sufficient evidence to prove equilibrium. We demonstrate this in a large-$N$ large-$q$ Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev (SYK) quench protocol. Although all post-quench fermion correlators are exactly thermal, a second quench back to the initial Hamiltonian reveals hidden memory of the initial state. It is encoded in correlators connecting to times before the first quench. This memory is an extensive nonequilibrium (NEQ) witness with its decay rate being the Lyapunov exponent $λ_L$. Thus $λ_L$ sets the rate at which the state becomes effectively indistinguishable from a Gibbs state. Despite being a unitary interacting many-body system, the complete NEQ real-time evolution is obtained exactly in the large-$N$, large-$q$ limit, making the setup ideal for analytically studying thermalization.

</details>


### [113] [Engineering Weak Universality with Quantum Dots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20755)
*Warre Missiaen,Michael Wimmer,Natalia Chepiga*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出利用量子点结构实现量子Ashkin-Teller模型与XYZ/八顶点模型，通过谐振腔介导和直接库仑相互作用，实现对连续变化临界指数的微观调控，并证明在短链中即可分辨其临界行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具有连续变化临界指数的量子临界理论在实验上难以获取，缺乏可控的微观实现平台。

Method: 提出量子点架构，分别利用谐振腔介导相互作用和直接库仑相互作用实现量子Ashkin-Teller模型与XYZ/八顶点模型，聚焦由非局域对偶性关联的Ashkin-Teller与八顶点临界线。

Result: 该平台可微观调控控制临界指数的参数，库仑实现支持更强相互作用区域；在短链中即可分辨连续变化的临界行为。实验可行参数下，Ashkin-Teller模型临界指数变化约10%，八顶点模型变化显著更大，且均远高于估计测量不确定度。

Conclusion: 量子点架构为实验研究连续变化临界指数的量子临界理论提供了可行途径，其可观测效应超出当前测量精度。

Abstract: Quantum critical theories with continuously varying critical exponents remain challenging to access experimentally. Here, we propose quantum-dot architectures for realizing the quantum Ashkin-Teller and XYZ/eight-vertex models using resonator-mediated and direct Coulomb interactions, respectively. We focus on the Ashkin-Teller and eight-vertex critical lines, which are connected by a non-local duality relating local order parameters to topological string operators. The resulting platforms provide microscopic control over the parameters explicitly controlling critical exponents, with the Coulomb-based implementation enabling stronger interaction regimes. We demonstrate that continuously varying critical behavior can already be resolved in short chains. For experimentally realistic parameters, the accessible interaction range is expected to produce changes in the critical exponents of order $10\%$ for the Ashkin-Teller model and substantially larger changes for the eight-vertex model. In both cases, the predicted variations lie well above the estimated measurement uncertainties.

</details>


### [114] [Spacetime Dynamics of Altermagnetic Magnons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20767)
*Ali Emami Kopaei,Karthik Subramaniam Eswaran,Krzysztof Wohlfeld*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文用线性自旋波理论研究二维棋盘格晶格中局域自旋激发的实空间传播，发现交错磁序会产生方向依赖的磁振子群速度与分支劈裂，形成"近似圆形外波前 + 内部十字形各向异性结构"的动力学指纹，可作为区别于铁磁/反铁磁序的时空探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区分交错磁性与常规铁磁、反铁磁序通常依赖动量空间探针（如能带劈裂、谱学测量）；作者希望寻找一种动量空间之外的互补判据，即实空间自旋激发的传播动力学，是否携带交错磁序的独特信息。

Method: 在含最近邻 J_1 与次近邻 J_2 耦合的二维棋盘格晶格上采用线性自旋波理论，计算局域自旋激发在实空间的时空传播，分析磁振子群速度的方向依赖性与两支磁振子分支的劈裂，并比较 J_2>0 与 J_2<0（非阻挫区）两种情形。

Result: 有限 J_2 导致方向依赖的磁振子群速度并使两支磁振子分支劈裂；整体传播更接近反铁磁而非铁磁，保留近似圆形的外波前，但波前内部出现显著的十字形空间结构。将 J_2 变号进入非阻挫区（J_2<0）会提高特征传播速度，并交换增强传播的对角方向，即各向异性空间图样旋转 π/2。

Conclusion: 交错磁磁振子的时空动力学具有可辨识的实空间传播特征，可作为动量空间探针的互补手段，为在固态与合成量子体系中识别非常规磁序提供新路径。

Abstract: Distinguishing altermagnetism from conventional ferromagnetism and antiferromagnetism typically relies on momentum-space probes. Here, we show that the real-space spreading of a localized spin excitation provides a distinctive dynamical fingerprint of altermagnetic order. Using linear spin-wave theory on a two-dimensional checkerboard lattice with nearest-neighbor and next-nearest-neighbor couplings J_1 and J_2, we demonstrate that finite J_2 produces direction-dependent magnon group velocities and splits the two magnon branches. The resulting propagation remains closer to that of an antiferromagnet than a ferromagnet, retaining an approximately circular outer wavefront, while the direction-dependent magnon velocities produce a pronounced cross-like spatial structure inside this front. We further show that changing the sign of J_2 to enter the unfrustrated regime (J_2<0) increases the characteristic propagation velocities and interchanges the diagonal directions of enhanced propagation, corresponding to a pi/2 rotation of the anisotropic spatial pattern. These results establish spacetime dynamics as a complementary probe of altermagnetic magnons, providing a route to identifying unconventional magnetic order through real-space propagation patterns in solid-state and synthetic quantum systems.

</details>


### [115] [Statistics, 't Hooft Anomaly, and the Else-Nayak Index: a careful comparison of concepts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20813)
*Hanyu Xue*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文指出广义对称性、拓扑激发统计与对称性反常之间的关系常被错误建立，提出以"拓扑激发跳跃算符与对称变换对易"作为更稳健的判据，并在适当假设下给出统计与反常的一一对应，最后通过高维DW规范场耦合从gauging视角解释该关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文献中普遍认为广义对称性、拓扑激发及其统计与对称性反常密切相关，但这一关系的建立方式存在隐患。例如将对称变换截断到有限区域得到的"对称片算符"虽在边界产生对称缺陷，几何图像类似跳跃算符，却无法给出良定义的统计，也不保证与对称反常一致。因此需要一个更自然、更可靠的框架来厘清二者关系并纠正文献中的误解。

Method: 1）批评"对称片算符"进路：有限区域截断对称变换只在边界产生缺陷，缺乏良定义统计；2）提出替代判据：拓扑激发的跳跃算符应是对称的，即与对称变换对易；3）在适当假设下，由该对易条件导出统计与反常的一一对应；4）将边界物质与高一维的DW规范场耦合，从gauging角度解释：跳跃算符本质上是gauging后作用于物理自由度的规范不变算符，固定规范场背景后，全局对称性来自保持该背景的规范变换，而跳跃的对称性条件恰是剩余规范不变性。

Result: 论证了对称片算符方法不足以保证与对称反常一致；证明了在适当假设下，跳跃算符的对称性条件可给出拓扑激发统计与对称反常之间的一一对应；并通过高一维DW规范场耦合的gauging图像，将跳跃算符解释为规范不变算符，使统计—反常对应关系获得规范理论层面的自然解释。

Conclusion: 对称性与拓扑激发的关系应通过"跳跃算符对称性"而非"对称片算符"来建立；该判据在适当假设下实现统计与反常的一一对应，澄清了文献中的潜在误解，为比较对称性与拓扑激发提供了更可靠的框架。

Abstract: Generalized symmetries and topological excitations, as well as symmetry anomalies and the statistics of topological excitations, are widely believed to be related. There are, however, pitfalls in how this relation is established. A lattice truncation of a symmetry transformation to a finite patch gives a symmetry patch operator that creates symmetry defects at its boundary. This geometric picture resembles a hopping operator creating topological excitations at the boundary of its support, but does not provide well-defined statistics, let alone guarantee agreement with the symmetry anomaly. A more natural and robust relation is that the hopping operators of topological excitations are symmetric: they commute with symmetry transformations. Under suitable assumptions, this condition yields a one-to-one correspondence between statistics and anomalies. We further couple boundary matter to a DW gauge field in one higher dimension to explain this relation from the perspective of gauging. A hopping operator is, in essence, a gauge-invariant operator acting on the physical degrees of freedom after gauging. Once the gauge-field background is fixed, global symmetry comes from gauge transformations that preserve that background, while the symmetric condition on hopping is precisely the remaining gauge invariance. This distinction clarifies potential misconceptions in the literature and provides a more reliable framework for comparing symmetries and topological excitations.

</details>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [116] [Universal Suppression of Dissipation across Conformal Interface in Open Quantum Critical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19292)
*Ruhanshi Barad,Tian Wu,Qicheng Tang,Xueda Wen*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究含共形界面的局部耗散量子临界链中的弛豫行为，提出"模式分辨弛豫系数"以消除原弛豫系数的非普适性歧义，并证明其在弱耗散下具有界面的普适依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形界面在封闭一维量子临界系统中可用普适物理量刻画能量与信息传输，但在耗散动力学中界面所起的作用尚不清楚。已有工作基于Liouvillian gap定义的弛豫系数在某些边界条件下会因界面透射率变化导致最小衰减率模式切换而失去普适性，需要一种消除歧义的刻画方式。

Method: 对含共形界面的临界谐振子链与临界自由费米子链，结合解析与数值计算，通过将同一Liouvillian rapidity模式随界面透射率连续追踪，定义模式分辨弛豫系数 $c_{\rm relax}$，并研究其对边界条件、耗散强度及局域耗散位置的依赖性。

Result: 原基于Liouvillian gap的弛豫系数在部分边界条件下非普适，原因是决定最小衰减率的模式会随界面透射率改变。模式分辨的 $c_{\rm relax}$ 在弱耗散区对所有考虑情形均表现出相同的、对界面透射率的普适依赖，与边界条件、耗散强度、局域耗散位置等微观细节无关；对边界耗散，该普适行为在有限耗散强度下依然成立。

Conclusion: 研究建立了开放量子临界系统中跨共形界面弛豫的普适模式分辨刻画，揭示了共形界面在耗散动力学中的普适传输特征。

Abstract: Conformal interfaces provide an important setting for studying universal transmission phenomena in one-dimensional quantum critical systems. While energy and information transmission across such interfaces are characterized by universal quantities in closed systems, the corresponding role of conformal interfaces in dissipative dynamics is less understood. In this work, we study relaxation in locally dissipative quantum critical chains with a conformal interface and show that a universal characterization emerges at the level of individual relaxation modes. We first show that the relaxation coefficient defined in the recent work [1] from the Liouvillian gap can become non-universal for certain boundary conditions because the mode determining the smallest decay rate can change as the interface transmission is varied. To resolve this ambiguity, we introduce a mode-resolved relaxation coefficient $c_{\rm relax}$ by continuously tracking the same Liouvillian rapidity mode as a function of the interface transmission. Using analytical and numerical calculations for a critical harmonic chain and a critical free-fermion chain, we find that, in the weak-dissipation regime, $c_{\rm relax}$ follows the same universal dependence on the interface transmission in all cases considered, independent of microscopic details such as the boundary conditions, dissipation strength, and location of the local dissipation. For boundary dissipation, this universal behavior persists even at finite dissipation strength. Our results establish a universal mode-resolved characterization of relaxation across conformal interfaces in open quantum critical systems.

</details>


### [117] [Collective Symmetry and Criticality from Single-Component Fluctuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19629)
*Chunhao Guo,Zhe Wang,Zheng Yan*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 利用单一序参量场产生的 Lee–Yang 零点比值，可从可及的单一分量中提取集体对称性与临界信息；在对称破缺相中零点比收敛于由集体分量数决定的无参数 Bessel 序列，在量子临界点附近涨落导致比值偏离并携带临界信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对称性对相的分类与临界系统有效理论的构建至关重要，但识别集体对称性存在困难：基于预设序参量的检验会遗漏隐藏分量。核心目标是从单一可及分量中同时提取对称性与临界信息。

Method: 以单一序参量场生成的 Lee–Yang 零点比值为工具，因为未被测量的分量会塑造投影涨落分布，从而改变零点的相对位置。结合解析推导与大规模量子蒙特卡洛模拟，研究具有常规及扩展连续对称性的多体系统。

Result: 在自发对称破缺相深处，各向同性序参量的零点比解析地趋近一个无参数 Bessel 序列，该序列由集体分量数目决定；在量子临界点附近，涨落使该分布发生形变，由此产生的比值偏差及其有限尺寸耦合依赖携带额外临界信息。量子蒙特卡洛模拟验证了上述结论。

Conclusion: 该方法无需测量所有竞争序参量即可定量获取集体对称性与临界性，为从单一可及分量中诊断隐藏对称成分提供了途径。

Abstract: Symmetry plays a fundamental role in modern physics by guiding the classification of phases and the construction of effective theories of critical systems. Identifying collective symmetry remains challenging because tests based on preselected order parameters can overlook hidden components. A central goal is therefore to extract symmetry and critical information from a single accessible component. We pursue this goal using Lee--Yang zero ratios generated by a single ordering field. Unmeasured components shape the projected fluctuation distribution and hence the relative zero positions. Deep in a phase with spontaneous symmetry breaking, we show analytically that the zero ratios for an isotropic order parameter approach a parameter-free Bessel sequence fixed by the number of collective components. Near quantum criticality, fluctuations deform this distribution. The resulting ratio deviations and their finite-size coupling dependence carry additional critical information. We demonstrate these results with large-scale quantum Monte Carlo simulations of many-body systems with conventional and enlarged continuous symmetries. This approach provides quantitative access to collective symmetry and criticality without measuring every competing order.

</details>


### [118] [Encounter Propagator for Multiple Targets: A Dirichlet-to-Neumann Spectral Formalism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19995)
*Denis S. Grebenkov*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文建立了多目标有界域受限扩散的碰遇（encounter）表述，通过将DtN算子分解为目标受限块并对各目标上的边界局部时分别刻画，结合拉普拉斯反演得到收敛的切换展开与算子值更新方程；对双目标情形给出显式谱表示，并在耦合保持谱模式时用修正贝塞尔函数精确重求和，另提出低秩近似与有效双模约化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目标碰遇表述中目标投影算子与Dirichlet-to-Neumann（DtN）算子可交换，从而其本征基可对角化局部时依赖；但多目标时各目标投影算子一般不与DtN算子交换，导致原有谱方法失效，需要新的分解与展开框架来刻画多目标受限扩散及各目标局部时的累积。

Method: 将DtN算子分解为目标受限块，分别处理各目标上累积的边界局部时；经多维拉普拉斯反演构造收敛的切换展开，其逐项对应目标间交替扩散转移；推导等价的算子值更新（renewal）方程；双目标时对角化目标受限块，以两个DtN块谱与目标间耦合矩阵给出显式谱表示；耦合保持谱模式时对切换级数用修正贝塞尔函数精确重求和；对分离目标利用非对角DtN块的平滑性采用低秩有限维近似，并研究小且良好分离目标的效双模约化。

Result: 获得多目标受限扩散的收敛切换展开与等价算子值更新方程；双目标情形得到由两个DtN块谱和耦合矩阵构成的显式谱表示，耦合保持谱模式时实现精确重求和；确认非对角DtN块对分离目标具平滑性，可用低秩有限维近似；在小且良好分离目标的有效双模约化中识别出一个目标间重复转移逐步被抑制的参数区域。

Conclusion: 该工作推广了碰遇表述至多目标受限扩散，绕过DtN算子与目标投影算子不可交换的困难，为多目标间扩散转移与局部时依赖提供可计算的谱与级数工具，并讨论其概率解释及对扩散控制反应的意义。

Abstract: We develop an encounter-based formulation of restricted diffusion in a bounded domain with multiple targets, resolving separately the boundary local time accumulated on each target. Unlike the single-target problem, the target projection operators do not generally commute with the governing Dirichlet-to-Neumann (DtN) operator, so that its eigenbasis does not diagonalize the local-time dependence. We overcome this difficulty by decomposing the DtN operator into target-restricted blocks. Multi-dimensional Laplace inversion then yields a convergent switching expansion, whose successive terms describe alternating diffusive transfers between the targets. We also derive an equivalent operator-valued renewal equation. For two targets, diagonalizing the target-restricted blocks provides an explicit spectral representation in terms of two DtN block spectra and an inter-target coupling matrix. When the coupling preserves spectral modes, the switching series can be resummed exactly in terms of modified Bessel functions. In general, as the off-diagonal DtN block is smoothing for separated targets, one can resort to low-rank finite-dimensional approximations. We examine an effective two-mode reduction for small, well-separated targets and identify a regime in which repeated inter-target transfers are progressively suppressed. Probabilistic interpretations and implications for diffusion-controlled reactions are discussed.

</details>


### [119] [Higher-Order Interactions in Complex Systems: Mechanisms, Behaviour, Representation and Reducibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20142)
*Francisco J. Pérez-Reche*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该综述区分了高阶互作的"机制、行为与表示"三类问题，系统梳理直接多体机制、非线性成对机制及间接路径，并形式化"可约性"框架，评述信息论与推断方法在检测高阶依赖中的作用，指出观测数据难以独立确立机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶互作能产生爆炸性同步、不连续相变等显著集体行为，但这类行为或"多体描述"本身并不能确立多体机制，因为二者都依赖于系统被如何表示。因此需要厘清机制、行为与表示之间的关系，避免将表示层面的多体性误认为机制层面的多体性。

Method: 本文将问题分解为三个层次：机制（支配系统的机制是什么）、行为（产生何种行为）、表示（包含哪些变量及关系如何编码）。在此基础上考察三类机制路径：直接多体机制、可模拟多体效果的非线性成对机制、经由共享环境或消去变量的间接路径，并将时间聚合归为观测假象而非机制。随后形式化可约性问题：将高阶模型能否被低阶模型替代，定义为源模型与一类可容许低阶模型之间的比较，并相对于目标（微观规则、可观测量或定性特征）和容差进行判定。方法上综述信息论与推断方法，用于检测高阶依赖、检验低阶替代模型并重构交互结构。

Result: 分析表明：相同行为可由不同机制产生，而某一特定高阶机制并不具有普遍的行为后果；因此机制与行为之间不存在一一对应关系。信息论与推断方法可作为互补工具用于检测高阶依赖、检验低阶替代并重构交互结构，但仅凭观测数据一般无法确立机制。

Conclusion: 本文提出以"可约性"为核心的组织框架，强调须明确目标与容差才能讨论高阶模型的低阶替代。未来开放问题包括：将机制与可测特征相联系的签名识别、粗粒化、可辨识性与因果性，以及将约化理论从平均场推广到更一般的场景。

Abstract: Higher-order interactions couple three or more elements in ways that cannot be decomposed into pairwise contributions. In diverse complex systems they produce striking collective behaviours such as explosive synchronization, discontinuous transitions and altered stability. Neither such behaviour nor a description written in many-body terms establishes a many-body mechanism, since both depend on how the system is represented. This review distinguishes three questions: what mechanism governs the system, what behaviour does it produce, and how is either represented? Representation covers both which variables are included and how relations among them are encoded. We examine direct many-body mechanisms, nonlinear but pairwise mechanisms that mimic them, and indirect routes through shared environments or eliminated variables, together with temporal aggregation, an artefact of observation rather than a mechanism. The same behaviour can arise from different mechanisms, and a given higher-order mechanism has no universal behavioural consequence. We then formulate reducibility, the question of whether a higher-order model can be replaced by a lower-order one, as a comparison between a source model and an admissible class of lower-order models, relative to a target and a tolerance. The target may be a microscopic rule, an observable or a qualitative feature. Information-theoretic and inferential methods are reviewed as complementary tools for detecting higher-order dependence, testing lower-order alternatives and reconstructing interaction structure. However, observational data alone generally cannot establish the mechanism. We close by identifying open problems in linking mechanisms to measurable signatures, coarse-graining, identifiability and causality, and in extending reductions beyond mean field. (Abridged)

</details>


### [120] [Landau diamagnetism and the de Haas-van Alphen effect from a single geometric construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20146)
*Sung-Hoon Lee*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 提出一种零温初等构造，把自由电子气占据态沿磁场方向逐区间归入朗道能级，用三角形间态转移与质心算术导出朗道抗磁性，并在费米面极值截面附近自然给出 dHvA 振荡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统朗道抗磁性由巨正则势导出，虽得正确磁化率但缺乏物理直观；而"极值截面决定 de Haas-van Alphen 振荡"这一规则通常只是断言，未被直观展示。作者希望给出一个既简单又能同时揭示两者的构造。

Method: 在零温下，将自由电子气的占据态沿磁场方向按区间分组，归入它们所凝聚到的朗道能级。在每个区间内，场致能量代价化为两个全等三角形之间的态转移，再由质心算术求得朗道磁化率。对极值截面附近失效的区域，用零温直接态计数定位振荡来源。

Result: 该构造在非极值区域成功给出朗道磁化率；仅在费米面极值截面的狭窄邻域内失效，此处贡献以 dHvA 周期振荡，每个穿过极值截面的朗道能级对应一次振荡。零温态计数表明残余集中于最靠近极值截面的少数区间，远处区间贡献在峰值振幅尺度下可忽略。

Conclusion: 该构造以初等方式统一解释了朗道抗磁性与 dHvA 振荡的极值截面规则，表明后者源于极值截面附近少数区间的态计数残余，远区间贡献可忽略，从而把常被断言的规则变为可展示的结果。

Abstract: Landau diamagnetism is usually derived from the grand canonical potential, a calculation that yields the correct susceptibility but little physical insight, while the rule that extremal cross sections govern the de Haas-van Alphen (dHvA) oscillations is commonly asserted rather than exhibited. We present an elementary zero-temperature construction in which the occupied states of a free-electron gas are grouped, interval by interval along the field direction, into the Landau levels onto which they condense. Within each interval the field-induced energy cost reduces to a transfer of states between two congruent triangles, and center-of-mass arithmetic yields the Landau susceptibility. The construction fails only in a narrow neighborhood of an extremal cross section of the Fermi surface; there the contribution oscillates with the dHvA period, one oscillation for each Landau level that passes through the extremal cross section. Direct zero-temperature state counting locates the oscillation: the residual is concentrated in the few intervals nearest the extremal cross section, the summed contribution of the distant ones being negligible on the scale of the peak oscillation amplitude.

</details>


### [121] [Universal 1/f Noise in the Power Spectra of Energy Time-series in Solvated DNA Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20391)
*Harsh Sahu,Deepika Sardana,Pramod Kumar,Ajay Kumar Chand,Sobhan Sen,Sanjay Puri*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 通过三种DNA构象的MD模拟，发现DNA自身、DNA-水及DNA-离子相互作用能时间序列普遍呈1/f噪声，衰减指数β≈0.90；DNA拓扑与结构刚性通过调节与水和反离子的动力学耦合，决定低频模式的强弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究DNA拓扑结构与环境耦合如何影响其能量涨落的随机行为，理解生物相关DNA构象的动力学特性。

Method: 对三种构象（G-四链体、发夹、双链）的溶剂化DNA进行全面的分子动力学（MD）模拟，分析DNA自身、DNA-水、DNA-离子相互作用能时间序列的噪声谱。

Result: 三类相互作用能时间序列均呈1/f噪声，具有普适衰减指数β≈0.90；柔性结构（G-四链体、发夹）的DNA自身能量存在显著低频模式，而刚性双链则缺失。

Conclusion: DNA拓扑与结构刚性调节其与 hydration shell 及移动反离子的动态耦合，该耦合关键性地塑造1/f噪声谱，决定不同DNA构象的随机行为差异。

Abstract: We investigate energy fluctuations of solvated DNA in three conformations: G-quadruplex, Hairpin, and Duplex. Our comprehensive molecular dynamics (MD) simulations demonstrate that the time-series of interaction energies for DNA self, DNA-water and DNA-ion show 1/f noise with a universal decay exponent $β\simeq 0.90$. We show that DNA topology and structural rigidity modulate its dynamic coupling with the surrounding hydration shell and mobile counter-ions. This coupling critically shapes the 1/f noise profile: DNA self energies in flexible structures (G-quadruplex, Hairpin) display pronounced low-frequency modes absent in the rigid Duplex. These findings reveal how the interplay between DNA topology and environmental coupling governs different stochastic behavior in biologically relevant forms of DNA.

</details>


### [122] [Khinchin's ergodicity and typicality in statistical mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20433)
*Dario Lucente,Marco Baldovin,Giacomo Gradenigo,Angelo Vulpiani*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文重访Khinchin的观点，通过对可积系统（谐振链与Toda模型）的解析与数值研究，表明平衡态统计力学的核心预测（如Maxwell-Boltzmann分布）即使在没有动力学混沌的情况下也成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gibbs系综方法在描述多粒子系统平衡态方面极为成功，但其成功原因仍有争议。通常推导平衡态统计力学主要结果需要关于动力学遍历性的强假设，然而经验观察表明即使这些假设不成立，理论预测似乎依然有效，因此需要重新审视统计力学有效性的真正前提。

Method: 作者采纳Khinchin的观点，即统计力学有效性的唯一相关要素是系统自由度数足够大以及选取广延观测量，而与微观动力学细节无关，不要求存在动力学混沌。为此，他们针对两个经典可积系统——谐振链和Toda模型——进行了解析与数值分析。

Result: 在谐振链和Toda模型这两个可积系统中，即使缺乏动力学混沌，仍能观察到平衡态统计力学所预言的许多重要特征，例如Maxwell-Boltzmann分布。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持Khinchin的观点：统计力学的有效性并不依赖遍历性或动力学混沌，而主要取决于大自由度数和对广延观测量的选择，这为理解统计力学与动力学之间的关联提供了新视角。

Abstract: The formulation of statistical mechanics in terms of ensembles, originally proposed by Gibbs, has proved to be extremely effective in describing the equilibrium state of many-particle systems, in the most diverse contexts (critical phenomena, quantum mechanics, biophysics, just to mention a few). The reasons of this success are still debated, and the connection between dynamics and probability in large systems remains somehow elusive. Indeed, in order to derive the main results of equilibrium statistical mechanics, strong assumptions on the ergodicity of the dynamics are usually required. Nonetheless, empirical observations seem to suggest that the predictions of the theory hold true even when such assumptions are not verified. In this paper we reconsider the point of view put forward by Khinchin, stating that the only relevant ingredients for the validity of statistical mechanics are the large number of degrees of freedom in the system and the choice of extensive observables, irrespectively of the details of the microscopic dynamics. In particular, the presence of dynamical chaos is not required. To this aim, we discuss some analytical and numerical results on a couple of classical integrable systems, the harmonic chain and the Toda model, showing that many important features predicted by equilibrium statistical mechanics, as for instance Maxwell-Boltzmann distribution, are found even in absence of chaos.

</details>


### [123] [Formal Fluctuation-Response Relations for Non-Stationary Systems: The Dynamic Conjugate Variable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20525)
*Igor M. Sokolov*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于"动态共轭变量"的简单方法，推导非平稳动力学下的涨落-响应关系（FRR），仅依赖马尔可夫性与转移概率密度对恒定扰动强度的可微性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 涨落-响应关系通常基于平稳性假设建立，但非平稳（如弛豫过程）下其形式尚需更简洁统一的推导，以厘清其成立的最小条件。

Method: 引入与扰动共轭的变量（动态共轭），采用接近标准推导的途径；仅假设底层动力学具有马尔可夫性，且过程转移PDF对时间无关的恒定扰动强度可微。该共轭变量为双时间量，在平稳情形下退化为通常的单时间共轭。

Result: 给出了非平稳动力学FRR的简洁推导；证明该双时间共轭结构在平稳极限下还原为已知的单时间形式。

Conclusion: 所提框架统一并复现了若干已知结果，表明FRR的有效性条件可弱化至马尔可夫性与转移PDF可微性。

Abstract: Fluctuation-response relations (FRRs) connect the linear response of a system to an external perturbation with properties of spontaneous fluctuations in the unperturbed system. We provide a simple derivation of FRRs for non-stationary dynamics following an almost standard way based on introducing a variable conjugated to perturbation (a dynamic conjugate). The derivation relies solely on the Markovianity of the underlying dynamics, with the only additional assumption that the transition PDF of the process is differentiable with respect to the strength of a constant, time- independent perturbation. The structure of this conjugate variable is however unusual: it is a two-time one, and reduces to a usual single-time conjugate in stationary situations. We show how several known results follow from this approach.

</details>


### [124] [Martingale theory for heat and phase-space contraction in heterogeneous diffusions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20681)
*Jing Qin,Nariya Uchida,Édgar Roldán*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究多维非平衡过阻尼Langevin动力学中热耗散的鞅性质，给出瞬态鞅/下鞅/上鞅的定量判据，并建立停时热统计与相空间收缩泛函的联系，应用于受重力与双电层力作用的球形微观粒子并经验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究非平衡随机能量学中热耗散的统计性质，特别是其与过程非线性特征之间的联系，以拓展随机能量学的适用范围。

Method: 针对一般性多维非平衡过阻尼Langevin动力学，允许输运系数显式依赖时间与空间，通过推导定量判据来判定热耗散的瞬态鞅、下鞅与上鞅性质；并将停时（如首次通过时间）的热统计与刻画相空间收缩的泛函相联系。

Result: 获得了判定热耗散呈现瞬态鞅、下鞅、上鞅性质的定量判据；揭示了停时热统计与相空间收缩泛函之间的紧密联系；对受重力与双电层力并和水动力相互作用的刚性壁附近球形微观粒子的热耗散统计性质做出预测，数值模拟验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了热涨落统计性质与非平衡过程非线性特征之间的非平凡关系，拓展了随机能量学的研究范围。

Abstract: We investigate martingale properties of heat dissipated by generic non-equilibrium overdamped Langevin dynamics with multiple dimensions and transport coefficients that may depend explicitly on time and/or space. We find quantitative criteria that establish that heat dissipation may exhibit transient martingale (time-conserved), submartingale (time increasing), and supermartingale (time decreasing) properties in Langevin dynamics with drift coefficients with spatial non-linear dependencies. Furthermore, we reveal a tight link between the heat statistics at stopping (e.g. first-passage) times with that of a functional quantifying the phase-space contraction in the system dynamics. The theoretical results are used to predict the statistical properties of the heat dissipated by a spherical microscopic particle subject to gravity and double-layer forces and hydrodynamically interacting with a rigid wall, whose validity is confirmed by numerical simulations. These results extend the scope of stochastic energetics by revealing a non-trivial relation between heat fluctuations' statistical properties and the non-linear features of non-equilibrium processes.

</details>


### [125] [Global Minima of the Thomson Problem in a Disk: A Molecular Dynamics Approach with Fixed Border Charges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20777)
*Georgiy K. Lavrov,Eduard G. Nikonov*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文报告了圆盘约束下经典Thomson问题在N=60、61、92和99时的改进全局最小构型，通过QMD方法结合固定边界启发式获得了更低能量的构型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Thomson问题中，圆盘约束下有限数目排斥库仑电荷的最低能量构型（全局最小）是长期未完全解决的问题，已有最优解在若干N值上可能并非真正全局最小，因此需要寻找能量更低的构型并验证其对称性与稳定性。

Method: 将淬火分子动力学（QMD）方法与Amore和Zarate提出的固定边界启发式相结合，系统搜索N=60、61、92、99时的低能构型，并分析Voronoi图与对称性。

Result: 获得能量分别为E_QMD(60)=2159.3584240930、E_QMD(61)=2237.19264190、E_QMD(92)=5358.35353314、E_QMD(99)=6254.83029083的构型，均优于此前最优值；N=60的Voronoi图与先前报道不同；确认N=61为C_2对称、N=99为D_1对称；N=92呈现清晰的D_1轴向缺陷对称，而此前报道的Voronoi图对称性较低。

Conclusion: 结果在多次独立运行中高度可复现，表明这些构型是稳健的全局最小候选解，为圆盘约束Thomson问题提供了更优的参考构型。

Abstract: We report improved global-minimum configurations for the classical Thomson problem of $N=60$, $61$, $92$, and $99$ repulsive Coulomb charges confined to a disk. By combining the quenched molecular dynamics (QMD) method with the fixed-border heuristic introduced by Amore and Zarate, we systematically obtain configurations with energies $E_{\mathrm{QMD}}(60)=2159.3584240930$, $E_{\mathrm{QMD}}(61)=2237.19264190$, $E_{\mathrm{QMD}}(92)=5358.35353314$, and $E_{\mathrm{QMD}}(99)=6254.83029083$, which improve upon the previously best-known values. For $N=60$, the Voronoi diagram of our configuration differs from the one reported earlier. The symmetries for $N=61$ $(C_{2})$ and $N=99$ $(D_{1})$ are confirmed and are consistent with the known symmetry patterns for these configurations. For $N=92$, whereas the previously reported Voronoi diagram has lower symmetry, our solution exhibits a clear $D_{1}$ (axial) symmetry of defects. The results are highly reproducible across multiple independent runs, providing strong evidence that these configurations are robust global-minimum candidates.

</details>
